For a two-student family portal, build the **Core** items first. **Standard** items are what schools use by default. **Advanced** items are what universities add, and most are optional for you.

## 1. Result publishing

**Core**
- Draft, review, publish and unpublish for each result
- Publish by student, by subject, by exam, or all at once
- Result publish date and time (scheduled release)
- Marks entry per subject and per question
- Pass mark and maximum mark per subject
- Result visible to the student only after publishing

**Standard**
- Result withholding, for example while a recheck is pending
- Rank and percentage calculation
- Printable or PDF mark statement
- Result history for each exam
- Locking marks after publishing, so edits need a reason
- Remarks from the COE per student and per subject

**Advanced**
- Revaluation and recheck reque

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

function searchDir(dir, pattern, maxDepth = 4, depth = 0) {
  if (depth > maxDepth) return;
  try {
    const list = fs.readdirSync(dir);
    for (const item of list) {
      if (item === 'node_modules' || item === '.git' || item === '.next') continue;
      const fullPath = path.join(dir, item);
      try {
        const stat = fs.statSync(fullPath);
        if (stat.isDirectory()) {
          searchDir(fullPath, pattern, maxDepth, depth + 1);
        } else if (stat.isFile() && (stat.size < 50000000)) {
          const content = fs.readFileSync(fullPath, 'utf8');
          if (content.includes('2104')) {
            const idx = content.indexOf('2104');
            const snippet = content.substring(Math.max(0, idx - 150), Math.min(content.length, idx + 250));
            if (snippet.toLowerCase().includes('roll') || snippet.toLowerCase().includes('regist') || snippet.toLowerCase().includes('format') || snippet.toLowerCase().includes('login') || snippet.toLowerCase().includes('student')) {
              console.log('FILE:', fullPath);
              console.log(snippet.replace(/[\r\n]+/g, ' '));
              console.log('---');
            }
          }
        }
      } catch (e) {}
    }
